4a1(rgyud): @#//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe/'gro lding ba'i gsang sngags

4b3(rgyud): re shwa te/pan d+ha ra ba si ni/ha ri ka ri ta/bing ka la k+Shi da d+hi mu khi/dus ma yin par 'chi ba thams cad las bdag bsrungs shig

5a3(rgyud): ldan 'das bdag bsrung ba bzhan gyis mi thub pa 'di/su yi snying la 'dzin tam/yi ger bris na/de la bdag gis rtag par rgyun tu 'jigs

5b1(rgyud): tad+ya ya thA/hi mi li mi li tsi li swAhA/pra ma swa ra swahA/pra ma gar+b+he pu Sh+pa swahA//bcom ldan 'das 'di ni bdag gi bsrung ba/tshangs pa'i tshad med pa zhes bya ba ste/dus ma 
5b2(rgyud): lags par 'gum pa thams cad las yongs su skyob pa/sdig pa thams cad rab tu zhi bar bgyid pa lags so//de nas lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis bcom ldan 'das ga la der 
5b3(rgyud): logs su thal mo sbyar ba btud te/bcom ldan 'das la 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das bdag gis kyang chos kyi rnam grangs 'di la/gzungs kyis gsang sngag+sa 
5b4(rgyud): bzhi rnams bgyi'o//tad+ya thA/ni b+ha rA ni/ban d+ha ban d+ha/mA nang De/ma ti ma ti/ti ti/g+ho ri g+han d+ha ri/tsan Da li ma ti gi/pa ra la ne/h+ye nam pa r+ya ma da na ni/ma la 
5b5(rgyud): ni||da la mad te/tsa kra/ba kra pa ri shA bar ha swahA//btsun pa bcom ldan 'das 'di ni bangag gi rig sngags rdo rje'i lce 'bebs pa zhes bgyi ba ste//'jigs pa thams 
5b6(rgyud): cad las thar bar bgyid pa/dus ma lags (pa)'gum pa thams cad las yos su skyob pa lags so//de nas rgyal po chen po yul 'khor bsrung dang /'phags skyes po dang |

6b3(rgyud): rnams mi bsdugs par bgyid pa/gshod byed dang /byad dang /ro langs dang /bkra mi shis pa 'jig par byed pa mchis na/bcom ldan 'das 
6b4(rgyud): bdag cag klu'i skye gnas 'di nas 'byung ba dang /ser sna spang ba'i slad du thugs rtse bar dgongs te/de bzhes su gsol/de ci'i slad du zhe na/bcom

8a1(rgyud): @#/ O /pa thams cad rab tu sgreng bar 'gyur//ta d+ya thA/oM badzra pra ka ra/badzra ka ra/badzra tsa kra dang shA/b+ha ya nA ke/

9a3(rgyud): rtsa ba/'jig rten pa dang /'jig rten las 'das pa yang rung /gang ci yod pa de bla na med pa'i ye shes kyi 'bras bur gyur cig/bdag gis rnam par grol ba

9a5(rgyud): me|kAn te kA te/da me da me/dAn te dAn te/b+ha tre b+ha tre/su b+ha tre su b+ha te/tsan dre tsan dre/su tsan dre su tsan dre/tsan dra kir ni/tsan dra pa ti/ti tso pa ti/d+ha

10a1(rgyud): @#/ O /ba te mahAtab b+hi klu sad+d+her sar d+has/mahAtAtatsa b+ho d+he swatAsang g+he na/te na kha lu pu na/sa ma yen/b+ha ga bAn gam+b+hi ra baM b+ha saM na ma/

10a3(rgyud): ba lo ka ya ti sma/pany+tsa skan d+hAM sh+tsa swa b+ha ba shu n+yam bya ba lo ka ya tisma/a tha a yu/Sh+ma na shA ri pu tro bud+d+ha a nu b+ha be na/a r+ya ba lo ki te shwa raM/bo d+hi sat+ta mahA set+ta me

11a1(rgyud): @#/ O /sa mo nan tra/sarba du/kha pra sha ma no man tra/sa t+ya mi ma th+ya t+ya ta/pra dz+nyA pA ra mi tA yug to man tra mahA/ta d+ya thA/
11a2(rgyud): oM ga te ga te/pA ra ga te/spa ra saM ga te/b+ho d+hi swAhA/a baM shA ri pu tra gaM b+hi rA yaM pradz+nyA pA ra mi tA yaM shi k+Shi ta byaM/bo d+hi sat+ta na ma hA sa twe na/a tha kha lu 
11a3(rgyud): b+ha ga bA na/ta smra ta sam d+her b+hud thA ya/ar+ya ta a ba le ki ti shwe ra s+ya b+hi d+hi sat+ta s+ya mahA sa twa s+ya/su d+hu kar ma t+ta sA d+hu sA nad+hu sa d+hu/ku la pu tre/e ba me   
11a4(rgyud): ta ta/e bam gam b+hi ra ra yAM/pradz+nyA pA ra mi tA yaMtsa ri ta byaM/ya thA tA yA nir diSh+Ta ma/ta ta nu mo d+hi sarba ta thA ga tai ri ti/i dam bo tsa ta/b+ha ga bAn ta ta

11b6(rgyud): dbang phyug dang /zla 'od gzhon nur (gyur)pa gnyis la bka' stsal pa/rigs kyi bu dag 'jig rten gyi khams 'di'i pha rol gyi yang ches pha rol/'jig rten gyi kham+sa 
12a1(rgyud): @#//bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag gang ga'i klung grangs med pa'i bye ma snyed 'das pa na/tsan dan gyis dpal zhes bya ba'i 'jig rten 
12a2(rgyud): gyi khams yod de/de na de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas tsan dan gyi yan lag ces bya ba da ltar bzhugs te/'tsho zhing 
12a3(rgyud): bzhes la chos kyang sten to//gang 'jig rten gyi khams de'i sems can de dag thams cad dag ni phyir mi ldog par gyur pa sha stag ste/bcom ldan 'das de bzhin
